<analysis>**original_problem_statement:**
The user's primary goal is to refine and stabilize a fully offline medical analysis application, Nabeeh, deployed on a dedicated GPU server.

The project's key requirements are:
1.  **AI Engine & Model:** Utilize  with a  model for all AI tasks.
2.  **Core Functionality:** The application must analyze clinical notes to:
    *   Identify documented (principal, secondary) diagnoses.
    *   Infer undocumented diagnoses from lab results, medications, and clinical context.
    *   Generate correctly formatted, non-leading clarification queries for physicians.
    *   Provide a detailed, well-structured case summary.
    *   Understand and correctly interpret common medical abbreviations.
3.  **UI/UX & Workflow:**
    *   Ensure UI colors and branding (Nabeeh) are consistent across all pages.
    *   Fix the post-note-save workflow. The user expects to save a note, view it, and then click a button to trigger the AI analysis. The current flow is broken.
